Special formatting rules\n    if \"참석자수\" in repl:\n        repl[\"참석자수\"] = digits_only(data.get(\"참석자수\"))\n\n    if \"만족도\" in repl:\n        repl[\"만족도\"] = format_satisfaction(data.get(\"만족도\"))\n\n    # Overall opinion field\n    if \"종합의견\" in repl:\n        base = repl[\"종합의견\"].rstrip()\n        repl[\"종합의견\"] = f\"{base} 후속 심화반 검토 요망.\"\n\n    return repl\n\n\ndef qn_local(el) -> str:\n    return etree.QName(el).localname\n\n\ndef remove_layout_caches(paragraph_el) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Remove stale layout-cache elements under a modified <hp:p>.\"\"\"\n    removed = 0\n    # Remove any descendant whose local name contains 'lineseg' or 'cache'\n    to_remove = []\n    for el in paragraph_el.iter():\n        if el is paragraph_el:\n            continue\n        ln = qn_local(el).lower()\n        if \"lineseg\" in ln or \"cache\" in ln:\n            to_remove.append(el)\n\n    for el in to_remove:\n        parent = el.getparent()\n        if parent is not None:\n            parent.remove(el)\n            removed += 1\n\n    return removed\n\n\nPLACEHOLDER_RE = re.compile(r\"\\{\\{([^}]+)\\}\\}\")\n\n\ndef replace_in_paragraph(p_el, replacements: dict) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Replace placeholders within a paragraph.\n\n    Robust to placeholders spanning multiple <hp:t> nodes by concatenating\n    all <hp:t> text, replacing in the concatenated string, then placing the\n    final text into the first <hp:t> and clearing the rest.\n\n    Returns True if paragraph content changed.\n    \"\"\"\n    t_nodes = list(p_el.findall(\".//{*}t\"))\n    if not t_nodes:\n        return False\n\n    original = \"\".join([t.text or \"\" for t in t_nodes])\n    if \"{{\" not in original:\n        return False\n\n    def sub_fn(m):\n        key = m.group(1)\n        if key not in replacements:\n            raise ValueError(f\"No replacement provided for placeholder '{{{{{key}}}}}'\")\n        return replacements[key]\n\n    replaced = PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(sub_fn, original)\n\n    if replaced == original:\n        return False\n\n    # Preserve formatting by keeping first <hp:t> node and clearing the rest.\n    t_nodes[0].text = replaced\n    for t in t_nodes[1:]:\n        t.text = \"\"\n\n    return True\n\n\ndef process_xml_file(xml_path: str, replacements: dict) -> None:\n    parser = etree.XMLParser(remove_blank_text=False)\n    tree = etree.parse(xml_path, parser)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    modified_paras = 0\n    removed_caches_total = 0\n\n    for p_el in root.findall(\".//{*}p\"):\n        try:\n            changed = replace_in_paragraph(p_el, replacements)\n        except ValueError as e:\n            # Bubble up with file context\n            raise ValueError(f\"{xml_path}: {e}\")\n\n        if changed:\n            modified_paras += 1\n            removed_caches_total += remove_layout_caches(p_el)\n\n    # Preserve directory structure; if 'mimetype' exists, store first and uncompressed.\n    all_files = []\n    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(src_dir):\n        dirs.sort()\n        files.sort()\n        for fn in files:\n            full = os.path.join(root, fn)\n            rel = os.path.relpath(full, src_dir).replace(os.sep, \"/\")\n            all_files.append((rel, full))\n\n    # Order: mimetype first (if present), then others.\n    mimetype = [x for x in all_files if x[0] == \"mimetype\"]\n    others = [x for x in all_files if x[0] != \"mimetype\"]\n    ordered = mimetype + others\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(out_hwpx_path, \"w\") as z:\n        for rel, full in ordered:\n            if rel == \"mimetype\":\n                z.write(full, rel, compress_type=zipfile.ZIP_STORED)\n            else:\n                z.write(full, rel, compress_type=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED)\n\n\ndef validate_no_placeholders(src_dir: str) -> None:\n    bad = []\n    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(src_dir):\n        for fn in files:\n            if not fn.lower().endswith(\".xml\"):\n                continue\n            p = os.path.join(root, fn)\n            with open(p, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n                s = f.read()\n            if \"{{\" in s or \"}}\" in s:\n                bad.append(p)\n\n    if bad:\n        msg = \"\\n\".join(bad)\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Placeholders still remain in XML files:\\n{msg}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Validate placeholders removed\n    validate_no_placeholders(EXTRACT_DIR)\n\n    # 6. Preserve directory structure; if 'mimetype' exists, store first and uncompressed.\n    all_files = []\n    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(src_dir):\n        dirs.sort()\n        files.sort()\n        for fn in files:\n            full = os.path.join(root, fn)\n            rel = os.path.relpath(full, src_dir).replace(os.sep, \"/\")\n            all_files.append((rel, full))\n\n    # Order: mimetype first (if present), then others.\n    mimetype = [x for x in all_files if x[0] == \"mimetype\"]\n    others = [x for x in all_files if x[0] != \"mimetype\"]\n    ordered = mimetype + others\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(out_hwpx_path, \"w\") as z:\n        for rel, full in ordered:\n            if rel == \"mimetype\":\n                z.write(full, rel, compress_type=zipfile.ZIP_STORED)\n            else:\n                z.write(full, rel, compress_type=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED)\n\n\ndef validate_no_placeholders(src_dir: str) -> None:\n    bad = []\n    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(src_dir):\n        for fn in files:\n            if not fn.lower().endswith(\".xml\"):\n                continue\n            p = os.path.join(root, fn)\n            with open(p, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n                s = f.read()\n            if \"{{\" in s or \"}}\" in s:\n                bad.append(p)\n\n    if bad:\n        msg = \"\\n\".join(bad)\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Placeholders still remain in XML files:\\n{msg}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    #
